Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The precipitation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 406. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 600 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on precipitation structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 406. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 600. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any precipitation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 729. The projective resolution has length at most 45 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the precipitation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 729 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 45. QED.
